!Sample_data_processing = Non-uniquely mapped reads were removed

!Sample_data_processing = Non-uniquely mapped reads were removed
!Sample_data_processing = Rpkmforgenes (available at http://sandberg.cmb.ki.se/rnaseq/), options -readcount -fulltranscript -mRNAnorm -rmnameoverlap -u, was used to generate RPKM values and read counts. We used a file with unique positions from Storvall et al. 2013 PLOS ONE.

!Sample_type = SRA
!Sample_channel_count = 1
!Sample_source_name_ch1 = fibroblast (primary culture from tail)
!Sample_organism_ch1 = Mus musculus
!Sample_taxid_ch1 = 10090
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = strain: C57BL/6J(mother) x CAST/EiJ(father)
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = cross: first generation cross
!Sample_characteristics_ch1 = developmental stage: fibroblast (primary culture from tail)
!Sample_molecule_ch1 = polyA RNA
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = We used lysis buffer of the kit 'SMARTer Ultra Low Input RNA for Illumina Sequencing' from Clontech
!Sample_extract_protocol_ch1 = We used Smart-seq2, as described by Picelli et al. Nature methods 2013 (PMID 24056875)
!Sample_description = fibroblast 21 genotype CASTxC57
!Sample_data_processing = For expression values, we aligned reads using bowtie 0.12.7, option --best, against the genome and transcriptome (Ensembl, version 16 May 2011) with the genotype of both strains, in mm9 coordinates (but for Smart-seq2 samples we used rna-star against the genome)
!Sample_data_processing = Non-uniquely mapped reads were removed
!Sample_data_processing = Rpkmforgenes (available at http://sandberg.cmb.ki.se/rnaseq/), options -readcount -fulltranscript -mRNAnorm -rmnameoverlap -u, was used to generate RPKM values and read counts. We used a file with unique positions from Storvall et al. 2013 PLOS ONE.
!Sample_data_processing = For allelic ratios, we aligned using gsnap version 2012-07-20, with tolerance for known SNP positions  (but for Smart-seq2 samples we used rna-star)
!Sample_data_processing = We used samtools mpileup to extract allelic counts
